2026-10-17T14:17:57.6454398Z   UV_CACHE_DIR: /home/runner/work/_temp/setup-uv-cache
2026-10-17T14:17:57.6464836Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [profile] Lower concurrency to 176 and start prefill before the decode handshake

Runtime-only change on the measured image e29dfde4... against the
reference run 38945855851 (6521.98 good output tok/s, concurrency 192,
P3/D1 TP4/DP4/EP4, decode cap 128, EAGLE3 steps 3 / draft 4,
total_requests dispatch on both roles). Decode cap, dispatch,
speculation, pool and image are unchanged; the decode dist-init port fix
from main is included. Two changes, both aimed at the 3.3% of requests
that fail the TTFT SLA (114 at the start burst, 26 in steady state) and
at nothing else, because the reference runtime is otherwise a local
optimum: cap 31, round-robin prefill, shorter speculation and the
warp-redux kernel all scored lower in runs 39019907035, 39035989837 and
39018872189.

1. Concurrency 192 -> 176. Little's law on the reference logs puts 75
   requests on the prefill side of TTFT; 16.8 of them sit in the decode
   waiting queue with KV already received, adding 0.49 s of TTFT and no
   throughput. Removing that queue trims steady TTFT by about 0.5 s
   (steady p99 2.68 s today) and the start burst by 16 requests, at a
   decode occupancy cost expected below 1%.

2. --optimistic-prefill-attempts 2 on the prefill workers. The prefill
   scheduler polls its bootstrap queue once per ~340 ms iteration, so a
   request waits a full iteration for the decode handshake; 55% of
   prefill batches launched with an empty waiting queue while requests
   waited there, and the GPUs were about 90% busy. The pinned SGLang
   forwards before the handshake, keeps KV in the request's own pages,
   and sends only after finalize_bootstrap receives the decode pages;
   two attempts suit the two-chunk requests; the repository patches do
   not touch the path (research note section 3.2). Outputs are
   semantically unchanged; batch composition changes, so bitwise
   reproducibility is not claimed and the official parser judges.

Checks after the run: server-info must show optimistic_prefill_attempts
2 on every prefill DP state and 0 on decode (the key is present in the
archived server-info of earlier runs); Prefill batch log lines should
show #optimistic-req > 0 and #bootstrap-req near 0; failure signatures
are "Prefill bootstrap failed", finalize assertions and growing prefill
#inflight-req. Expected score 6620-6720. Ruff, shell syntax and the full
test suite pass. Implementation by a delegated agent, reviewed before
submission.
